917 entries, grim-prompter to grok-crew-runtime. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **grim-prompter**: Threat model the current repository with Grim Prompter. Use when the user asks to threat model a project, find design-level security threats, map trust boundaries, run STRIDE, PASTA, LINDDUN or another threat modeling method, examine privacy risks or GDPR/CCPA coverage, challenge an existing threat model, or review how a code change affects one. Reads the project read-only and writes evidence-traceable JSON and Markdown reports to a threat-model/ folder. ([Reyansh-Niranjan/r2](https://github.com/Reyansh-Niranjan/r2/tree/HEAD/specialists/grim-prompter))
- **grim-prompter**: Pemodelan ancaman berbasis bukti (Grim Prompter) untuk Coder (Shikaku / Sir.
